{"forms":[{"form":"solicitors","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"solicitour","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Traditional solicitors are still found in the United Kingdom, Ireland, New Zealand, India, Hong Kong, Singapore, and parts of Australia.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["One who solicits","A lawyer offering most legal services to clients apart from representation in court, for which the lawyer typically solicits and instructs a barrister instead, although rights of audience vary by jurisdiction."],"synonyms":[{"word":"transactional attorney"}]},{"glosses":["One who solicits","A lawyer now performing both the role of a traditional solicitor and that of a barrister."],"tags":["Australia","Canada"]},{"glosses":["One who solicits","The chief legal officer of a city, town, or similar jurisdiction in some American states."],"tags":["US"]},{"glosses":["One who solicits","A customer, a requester of goods and services, especially johns, the clients of prostitutes."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Did you see the sign that says 'No Solicitors'?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["One who solicits","A door-to-door salesperson, a person soliciting sales by visiting others' houses."],"tags":["Canada","US"]},{"glosses":["One who solicits","An agent or proxy in other contexts, especially a petitioner, an intercessor, a go-between."],"tags":["archaic"]},{"glosses":["One who solicits","A catalyst, a thing prompting some action or behavior."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"glosses":["One who solicits","A tempter, an inciter."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"glosses":["One who solicits","A military recruiter."],"tags":["obsolete","rare"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/səˈlɪsɪtɚ/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/səˈlɪsɪtə/","tags":["UK"]},{"ipa":"/səˈlɪstə/","tags":["UK","contracted"]}],"upstream_line":340755,"upstream_offset":1654387798,"upstream_record_sha256":"827bdcaadab4066c5d4ea1f0cbc05ac7eb4fae66d703141df776b5ad48c2e888","word":"solicitor"}
